	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	business_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	customer_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,

	status VARCHAR(9) NOT NULL,

	PRIMARY KEY (id)

	pix_qr_url TEXT,

	description TEXT,

	last_notification_sent_at DATETIME,

CREATE TABLE recurrences (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	business_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	crm_contact_id UUID, 
	cooperado_id UUID, 
	category_id UUID, 
	description TEXT NOT NULL, 
	amount NUMERIC(20, 8) NOT NULL, 
	frequency VARCHAR(7) NOT NULL, 
	first_due_date DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	next_due_date DATETIME, 
	parcel_total INTEGER, 
	parcel_current INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	status VARCHAR(9) NOT NULL, 
	payment_method VARCHAR(11) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(business_id) REFERENCES businesses (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(crm_contact_id) REFERENCES crm_contacts (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(cooperado_id) REFERENCES cooperados (id) ON DELETE SET NULL, 
	FOREIGN KEY(category_id) REFERENCES account_categories (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
)

	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

	id UUID NOT NULL,

	customer_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	customer_phone VARCHAR(50), 
	customer_email VARCHAR(255), 
	customer_doc VARCHAR(20), 
	amount NUMERIC(20, 8) NOT NULL, 
	due_date DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	data_competencia DATETIME, 
	status VARCHAR(9) NOT NULL, 
	pix_code TEXT, 
	pix_qr_url TEXT, 
	payment_method VARCHAR(11) NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	project_id VARCHAR(100), 
	notification_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	last_notification_sent_at DATETIME, 
	viewed_at DATETIME, 
	remaining_balance NUMERIC(20, 8), 
	created_by_id VARCHAR(36), 
	created_by_name VARCHAR(255), 
	updated_by_id VARCHAR(36), 
	updated_by_name VARCHAR(255), 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(business_id) REFERENCES businesses (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(cooperado_id) REFERENCES cooperados (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(crm_contact_id) REFERENCES crm_contacts (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

CREATE TABLE recurrences (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	business_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	crm_contact_id UUID, 
	cooperado_id UUID, 
	category_id UUID, 
	description TEXT NOT NULL, 
	amount NUMERIC(20, 8) NOT NULL, 
	frequency VARCHAR(7) NOT NULL, 
	first_due_date DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	next_due_date DATETIME, 
	parcel_total INTEGER, 
	parcel_current INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	status VARCHAR(9) NOT NULL, 
	payment_method VARCHAR(11) NOT NULL,

	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL,

	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE businesses (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	user_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	cnpj VARCHAR(20), 
	category VARCHAR(11) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL,

	FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

CREATE TABLE privacy_requests (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	user_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	broker_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	status VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, 
	sent_at DATETIME, 
	last_checked_at DATETIME, 
	notes TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(broker_id) REFERENCES data_brokers (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	user_name VARCHAR(255),

	role VARCHAR(50),

	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE privacy_requests (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	user_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	broker_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	status VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, 
	sent_at DATETIME, 
	last_checked_at DATETIME, 
	notes TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(broker_id) REFERENCES data_brokers (id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)

	user_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,

	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	business_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	cooperado_id UUID, 
	crm_contact_id UUID, 
	category_id UUID, 
	invoice_type VARCHAR(22) NOT NULL, 
	customer_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	customer_phone VARCHAR(50), 
	customer_email VARCHAR(255), 
	customer_doc VARCHAR(20), 
	amount NUMERIC(20, 8) NOT NULL, 
	due_date DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	data_competencia DATETIME, 
	status VARCHAR(9) NOT NULL, 
	pix_code TEXT, 
	pix_qr_url TEXT, 
	payment_method VARCHAR(11) NOT NULL, 
	description TEXT, 
	project_id VARCHAR(100), 
	notification_count INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	last_notification_sent_at DATETIME, 
	viewed_at DATETIME, 
	remaining_balance NUMERIC(20, 8), 
	created_by_id VARCHAR(36), 
	created_by_name VARCHAR(255), 
	updated_by_id VARCHAR(36), 
	updated_by_name VARCHAR(255), 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(business_id) REFERENCES businesses (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(cooperado_id) REFERENCES cooperados (id) ON DELETE CASCADE, 
	FOREIGN KEY(crm_contact_id) REFERENCES crm_contacts (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

CREATE TABLE privacy_requests (
	id UUID NOT NULL, 
	user_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	broker_id UUID NOT NULL, 
	status VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, 
	sent_at DATETIME, 
	last_checked_at DATETIME, 
	notes TEXT, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

)

	business_id UUID NOT NULL,
